sort Board1;
     Row1;
     Piece = struct black | white | empty;
     Row = struct row(Piece, Piece, Piece, Piece, Piece);
     Board = struct col(Row, Row, Row, Row, Row);
     Enum3 = struct e2_3 | e1_3 | e0_3;

cons c_col: Board1;
     c_row: Row1;

map  differ1,differ2: Pos # Pos -> Bool;
     getPiece: Pos # Pos # Board -> Piece;
     setPiece: Pos # Pos # Piece # Board -> Board;
     getPiece: Pos # Row -> Piece;
     setPiece: Pos # Piece # Row -> Row;
     initialBoard,finalBoard: Board;
     isOccupied: Pos # Pos # Board -> Bool;
     isKnight'sMove: Pos # Pos # Pos # Pos -> Bool;
     C3_: Enum3 # Pos # Pos # Pos -> Pos;
     C3_1: Enum3 # Board # Board # Board -> Board;
     C3_2: Enum3 # Bool # Bool # Bool -> Bool;
     C_Board1: Board1 # Board -> Board;
     Det_Board1: Board -> Board1;
     C_Board1: Board1 # Board1 -> Board1;
     pi_Board1,pi_Board,pi_Board2,pi_Board3,pi_Board4: Board -> Row;
     C_Board1: Board1 # Row -> Row;
     C_Row1: Row1 # Row -> Row;
     Det_Row1: Row -> Row1;
     C_Row1: Row1 # Row1 -> Row1;
     pi_Row1,pi_Row,pi_Row2,pi_Row3,pi_Row4: Row -> Piece;
     C_Row1: Row1 # Piece -> Piece;

var  p,p': Pos;
     r1,r2,r3,r4,r5: Row;
     pc,p1,p2,p3,p4,p5: Piece;
eqn  differ1(p, p')  =  if(p > p', p - p' == 1, p' - p == 1);
     differ2(p, p')  =  if(p > p', p - p' == 2, p' - p == 2);
     getPiece(1, p, col(r1, r2, r3, r4, r5))  =  getPiece(p, r1);
     getPiece(2, p, col(r1, r2, r3, r4, r5))  =  getPiece(p, r2);
     getPiece(3, p, col(r1, r2, r3, r4, r5))  =  getPiece(p, r3);
     getPiece(4, p, col(r1, r2, r3, r4, r5))  =  getPiece(p, r4);
     getPiece(5, p, col(r1, r2, r3, r4, r5))  =  getPiece(p, r5);
     setPiece(1, p, pc, col(r1, r2, r3, r4, r5))  =  col(setPiece(p, pc, r1), r2, r3, r4, r5);
     setPiece(2, p, pc, col(r1, r2, r3, r4, r5))  =  col(r1, setPiece(p, pc, r2), r3, r4, r5);
     setPiece(3, p, pc, col(r1, r2, r3, r4, r5))  =  col(r1, r2, setPiece(p, pc, r3), r4, r5);
     setPiece(4, p, pc, col(r1, r2, r3, r4, r5))  =  col(r1, r2, r3, setPiece(p, pc, r4), r5);
     setPiece(5, p, pc, col(r1, r2, r3, r4, r5))  =  col(r1, r2, r3, r4, setPiece(p, pc, r5));
     setPiece(1, pc, row(p1, p2, p3, p4, p5))  =  row(pc, p2, p3, p4, p5);
     setPiece(2, pc, row(p1, p2, p3, p4, p5))  =  row(p1, pc, p3, p4, p5);
     setPiece(3, pc, row(p1, p2, p3, p4, p5))  =  row(p1, p2, pc, p4, p5);
     setPiece(4, pc, row(p1, p2, p3, p4, p5))  =  row(p1, p2, p3, pc, p5);
     setPiece(5, pc, row(p1, p2, p3, p4, p5))  =  row(p1, p2, p3, p4, pc);
     getPiece(1, row(p1, p2, p3, p4, p5))  =  p1;
     getPiece(2, row(p1, p2, p3, p4, p5))  =  p2;
     getPiece(3, row(p1, p2, p3, p4, p5))  =  p3;
     getPiece(4, row(p1, p2, p3, p4, p5))  =  p4;
     getPiece(5, row(p1, p2, p3, p4, p5))  =  p5;
     initialBoard  =  col(row(black, black, black, black, black), row(white, black, black, black, black), row(white, white, empty, black, black), row(white, white, white, white, black), row(white, white, white, white, white));
     finalBoard  =  col(row(white, white, white, white, white), row(black, white, white, white, white), row(black, black, empty, white, white), row(black, black, black, black, white), row(black, black, black, black, black));

var  p1,p2,q1,q2,x5,y3,y2,y1: Pos;
     f,x6,y6,y5,y4,d1,d8: Board;
     e1,e2,e3: Enum3;
     x7,y9,y8,y7,d7: Bool;
     d,d9: Board1;
     d2,d3,d4,d5,d6: Row;
eqn  isOccupied(p1, p2, f)  =  getPiece(p1, p2, f) != empty;
     isKnight'sMove(p1, p2, q1, q2)  =  differ1(p1, q1) && differ2(p2, q2) || differ2(p1, q1) && differ1(p2, q2);
     C3_(e1, x5, x5, x5)  =  x5;
     C3_(e2_3, y3, y2, y1)  =  y3;
     C3_(e1_3, y3, y2, y1)  =  y2;
     C3_(e0_3, y3, y2, y1)  =  y1;
     C3_1(e2, x6, x6, x6)  =  x6;
     C3_1(e2_3, y6, y5, y4)  =  y6;
     C3_1(e1_3, y6, y5, y4)  =  y5;
     C3_1(e0_3, y6, y5, y4)  =  y4;
     C3_2(e3, x7, x7, x7)  =  x7;
     C3_2(e2_3, y9, y8, y7)  =  y9;
     C3_2(e1_3, y9, y8, y7)  =  y8;
     C3_2(e0_3, y9, y8, y7)  =  y7;
     C_Board1(c_col, d1)  =  d1;
     C_Board1(d, d1)  =  d1;
     Det_Board1(col(d2, d3, d4, d5, d6))  =  c_col;
     Det_Board1(if(d7, d1, d8))  =  if(d7, Det_Board1(d1), Det_Board1(d8));
     C_Board1(c_col, d9)  =  d9;
     C_Board1(d, d9)  =  d9;
     C_Board1(d, c_col)  =  d;
     Det_Board1(C_Board1(d, d1))  =  C_Board1(d, Det_Board1(d1));
     pi_Board1(col(d2, d3, d4, d5, d6))  =  d2;
     pi_Board1(if(d7, d1, d8))  =  if(d7, pi_Board1(d1), pi_Board1(d8));
     C_Board1(c_col, d2)  =  d2;
     C_Board1(d, d2)  =  d2;
     pi_Board1(C_Board1(d, d1))  =  C_Board1(d, pi_Board1(d1));
     pi_Board(col(d2, d3, d4, d5, d6))  =  d3;
     pi_Board(if(d7, d1, d8))  =  if(d7, pi_Board(d1), pi_Board(d8));
     pi_Board(C_Board1(d, d1))  =  C_Board1(d, pi_Board(d1));
     pi_Board2(col(d2, d3, d4, d5, d6))  =  d4;
     pi_Board2(if(d7, d1, d8))  =  if(d7, pi_Board2(d1), pi_Board2(d8));
     pi_Board2(C_Board1(d, d1))  =  C_Board1(d, pi_Board2(d1));
     pi_Board3(col(d2, d3, d4, d5, d6))  =  d5;
     pi_Board3(if(d7, d1, d8))  =  if(d7, pi_Board3(d1), pi_Board3(d8));
     pi_Board3(C_Board1(d, d1))  =  C_Board1(d, pi_Board3(d1));
     pi_Board4(col(d2, d3, d4, d5, d6))  =  d6;
     pi_Board4(if(d7, d1, d8))  =  if(d7, pi_Board4(d1), pi_Board4(d8));
     pi_Board4(C_Board1(d, d1))  =  C_Board1(d, pi_Board4(d1));

var  d1,d8: Row;
     d,d9: Row1;
     d2,d3,d4,d5,d6: Piece;
     d7: Bool;
eqn  C_Row1(c_row, d1)  =  d1;
     C_Row1(d, d1)  =  d1;
     Det_Row1(row(d2, d3, d4, d5, d6))  =  c_row;
     Det_Row1(if(d7, d1, d8))  =  if(d7, Det_Row1(d1), Det_Row1(d8));
     C_Row1(c_row, d9)  =  d9;
     C_Row1(d, d9)  =  d9;
     C_Row1(d, c_row)  =  d;
     Det_Row1(C_Row1(d, d1))  =  C_Row1(d, Det_Row1(d1));
     pi_Row1(row(d2, d3, d4, d5, d6))  =  d2;
     pi_Row1(if(d7, d1, d8))  =  if(d7, pi_Row1(d1), pi_Row1(d8));
     C_Row1(c_row, d2)  =  d2;
     C_Row1(d, d2)  =  d2;
     pi_Row1(C_Row1(d, d1))  =  C_Row1(d, pi_Row1(d1));
     pi_Row(row(d2, d3, d4, d5, d6))  =  d3;
     pi_Row(if(d7, d1, d8))  =  if(d7, pi_Row(d1), pi_Row(d8));
     pi_Row(C_Row1(d, d1))  =  C_Row1(d, pi_Row(d1));
     pi_Row2(row(d2, d3, d4, d5, d6))  =  d4;
     pi_Row2(if(d7, d1, d8))  =  if(d7, pi_Row2(d1), pi_Row2(d8));
     pi_Row2(C_Row1(d, d1))  =  C_Row1(d, pi_Row2(d1));
     pi_Row3(row(d2, d3, d4, d5, d6))  =  d5;
     pi_Row3(if(d7, d1, d8))  =  if(d7, pi_Row3(d1), pi_Row3(d8));
     pi_Row3(C_Row1(d, d1))  =  C_Row1(d, pi_Row3(d1));
     pi_Row4(row(d2, d3, d4, d5, d6))  =  d6;
     pi_Row4(if(d7, d1, d8))  =  if(d7, pi_Row4(d1), pi_Row4(d8));
     pi_Row4(C_Row1(d, d1))  =  C_Row1(d, pi_Row4(d1));

act  jump,ready;

glob dc: Board;
     dc_pp: Board1;
     dc_pp1,dc_pp2,dc_pp3,dc_pp4,dc_pp5: Row;
     dc_pp6,dc_pp12,dc_pp18,dc_pp24,dc_pp30: Row1;
     dc_pp7,dc_pp8,dc_pp9,dc_pp10,dc_pp11,dc_pp13,dc_pp14,dc_pp15,dc_pp16,dc_pp17,dc_pp19,dc_pp20,dc_pp21,dc_pp22,dc_pp23,dc_pp25,dc_pp26,dc_pp27,dc_pp28,dc_pp29,dc_pp31,dc_pp32,dc_pp33,dc_pp34,dc_pp35: Piece;

proc P(s1_X: Pos, f_X_pp: Board1, f_X_pp1_pp: Row1, f_X_pp1_pp1,f_X_pp1_pp2,f_X_pp1_pp3,f_X_pp1_pp4,f_X_pp1_pp5: Piece, f_X_pp2_pp: Row1, f_X_pp2_pp1,f_X_pp2_pp2,f_X_pp2_pp3,f_X_pp2_pp4,f_X_pp2_pp5: Piece, f_X_pp3_pp: Row1, f_X_pp3_pp1,f_X_pp3_pp2,f_X_pp3_pp3,f_X_pp3_pp4,f_X_pp3_pp5: Piece, f_X_pp4_pp: Row1, f_X_pp4_pp1,f_X_pp4_pp2,f_X_pp4_pp3,f_X_pp4_pp4,f_X_pp4_pp5: Piece, f_X_pp5_pp: Row1, f_X_pp5_pp1,f_X_pp5_pp2,f_X_pp5_pp3,f_X_pp5_pp4,f_X_pp5_pp5: Piece) =
       (s1_X == 1 && (f_X_pp1_pp1 == white && f_X_pp1_pp2 == white && f_X_pp1_pp3 == white && f_X_pp1_pp4 == white && f_X_pp1_pp5 == white) && (f_X_pp2_pp1 == black && f_X_pp2_pp2 == white && f_X_pp2_pp3 == white && f_X_pp2_pp4 == white && f_X_pp2_pp5 == white) && (f_X_pp3_pp1 == black && f_X_pp3_pp2 == black && f_X_pp3_pp3 == empty && f_X_pp3_pp4 == white && f_X_pp3_pp5 == white) && (f_X_pp4_pp1 == black && f_X_pp4_pp2 == black && f_X_pp4_pp3 == black && f_X_pp4_pp4 == black && f_X_pp4_pp5 == white) && f_X_pp5_pp1 == black && f_X_pp5_pp2 == black && f_X_pp5_pp3 == black && f_X_pp5_pp4 == black && f_X_pp5_pp5 == black) ->
         ready .
         P(s1_X = 2, f_X_pp = dc_pp, f_X_pp1_pp = dc_pp30, f_X_pp1_pp1 = dc_pp31, f_X_pp1_pp2 = dc_pp32, f_X_pp1_pp3 = dc_pp33, f_X_pp1_pp4 = dc_pp34, f_X_pp1_pp5 = dc_pp35, f_X_pp2_pp = dc_pp24, f_X_pp2_pp1 = dc_pp25, f_X_pp2_pp2 = dc_pp26, f_X_pp2_pp3 = dc_pp27, f_X_pp2_pp4 = dc_pp28, f_X_pp2_pp5 = dc_pp29, f_X_pp3_pp = dc_pp18, f_X_pp3_pp1 = dc_pp19, f_X_pp3_pp2 = dc_pp20, f_X_pp3_pp3 = dc_pp21, f_X_pp3_pp4 = dc_pp22, f_X_pp3_pp5 = dc_pp23, f_X_pp4_pp = dc_pp12, f_X_pp4_pp1 = dc_pp13, f_X_pp4_pp2 = dc_pp14, f_X_pp4_pp3 = dc_pp15, f_X_pp4_pp4 = dc_pp16, f_X_pp4_pp5 = dc_pp17, f_X_pp5_pp = dc_pp6, f_X_pp5_pp1 = dc_pp7, f_X_pp5_pp2 = dc_pp8, f_X_pp5_pp3 = dc_pp9, f_X_pp5_pp4 = dc_pp10, f_X_pp5_pp5 = dc_pp11)
     + (s1_X == 1 && f_X_pp1_pp1 == empty && !((f_X_pp1_pp1 == white && f_X_pp1_pp2 == white && f_X_pp1_pp3 == white && f_X_pp1_pp4 == white && f_X_pp1_pp5 == white) && (f_X_pp2_pp1 == black && f_X_pp2_pp2 == white && f_X_pp2_pp3 == white && f_X_pp2_pp4 == white && f_X_pp2_pp5 == white) && (f_X_pp3_pp1 == black && f_X_pp3_pp2 == black && f_X_pp3_pp3 == empty && f_X_pp3_pp4 == white && f_X_pp3_pp5 == white) && (f_X_pp4_pp1 == black && f_X_pp4_pp2 == black && f_X_pp4_pp3 == black && f_X_pp4_pp4 == black && f_X_pp4_pp5 == white) && f_X_pp5_pp1 == black && f_X_pp5_pp2 == black && f_X_pp5_pp3 == black && f_X_pp5_pp4 == black && f_X_pp5_pp5 == black)) ->
         tau .
         P(s1_X = 1, f_X_pp = c_col, f_X_pp1_pp = c_row, f_X_pp1_pp1 = f_X_pp3_pp2, f_X_pp1_pp2 = f_X_pp1_pp2, f_X_pp1_pp3 = empty, f_X_pp1_pp4 = f_X_pp1_pp4, f_X_pp1_pp5 = f_X_pp1_pp5, f_X_pp2_pp = c_row, f_X_pp2_pp1 = f_X_pp2_pp1, f_X_pp2_pp2 = f_X_pp2_pp2, f_X_pp2_pp3 = f_X_pp2_pp3, f_X_pp2_pp4 = f_X_pp2_pp4, f_X_pp2_pp5 = f_X_pp2_pp5, f_X_pp3_pp = c_row, f_X_pp3_pp1 = f_X_pp3_pp1, f_X_pp3_pp2 = f_X_pp1_pp3, f_X_pp3_pp3 = f_X_pp3_pp3, f_X_pp3_pp4 = f_X_pp3_pp4, f_X_pp3_pp5 = f_X_pp3_pp5, f_X_pp4_pp = c_row, f_X_pp4_pp1 = f_X_pp4_pp1, f_X_pp4_pp2 = f_X_pp4_pp2, f_X_pp4_pp3 = f_X_pp4_pp3, f_X_pp4_pp4 = f_X_pp4_pp4, f_X_pp4_pp5 = f_X_pp4_pp5, f_X_pp5_pp = c_row, f_X_pp5_pp1 = f_X_pp5_pp1, f_X_pp5_pp2 = f_X_pp5_pp2, f_X_pp5_pp3 = f_X_pp5_pp3, f_X_pp5_pp4 = f_X_pp5_pp4, f_X_pp5_pp5 = f_X_pp5_pp5)
     + (s1_X == 1 && f_X_pp1_pp1 == empty && !((f_X_pp1_pp1 == white && f_X_pp1_pp2 == white && f_X_pp1_pp3 == white && f_X_pp1_pp4 == white && f_X_pp1_pp5 == white) && (f_X_pp2_pp1 == black && f_X_pp2_pp2 == white && f_X_pp2_pp3 == white && f_X_pp2_pp4 == white && f_X_pp2_pp5 == white) && (f_X_pp3_pp1 == black && f_X_pp3_pp2 == black && f_X_pp3_pp3 == empty && f_X_pp3_pp4 == white && f_X_pp3_pp5 == white) && (f_X_pp4_pp1 == black && f_X_pp4_pp2 == black && f_X_pp4_pp3 == black && f_X_pp4_pp4 == black && f_X_pp4_pp5 == white) && f_X_pp5_pp1 == black && f_X_pp5_pp2 == black && f_X_pp5_pp3 == black && f_X_pp5_pp4 == black && f_X_pp5_pp5 == black)) ->
         tau .
         P(s1_X = 1, f_X_pp = c_col, f_X_pp1_pp = c_row, f_X_pp1_pp1 = f_X_pp2_pp3, f_X_pp1_pp2 = f_X_pp1_pp2, f_X_pp1_pp3 = f_X_pp1_pp3, f_X_pp1_pp4 = f_X_pp1_pp4, f_X_pp1_pp5 = f_X_pp1_pp5, f_X_pp2_pp = c_row, f_X_pp2_pp1 = f_X_pp2_pp1, f_X_pp2_pp2 = f_X_pp2_pp2, f_X_pp2_pp3 = f_X_pp3_pp1, f_X_pp2_pp4 = f_X_pp2_pp4, f_X_pp2_pp5 = f_X_pp2_pp5, f_X_pp3_pp = c_row, f_X_pp3_pp1 = empty, f_X_pp3_pp2 = f_X_pp3_pp2, f_X_pp3_pp3 = f_X_pp3_pp3, f_X_pp3_pp4 = f_X_pp3_pp4, f_X_pp3_pp5 = f_X_pp3_pp5, f_X_pp4_pp = c_row, f_X_pp4_pp1 = f_X_pp4_pp1, f_X_pp4_pp2 = f_X_pp4_pp2, f_X_pp4_pp3 = f_X_pp4_pp3, f_X_pp4_pp4 = f_X_pp4_pp4, f_X_pp4_pp5 = f_X_pp4_pp5, f_X_pp5_pp = c_row, f_X_pp5_pp1 = f_X_pp5_pp1, f_X_pp5_pp2 = f_X_pp5_pp2, f_X_pp5_pp3 = f_X_pp5_pp3, f_X_pp5_pp4 = f_X_pp5_pp4, f_X_pp5_pp5 = f_X_pp5_pp5)
     + (s1_X == 1 && f_X_pp1_pp3 == empty && !((f_X_pp1_pp1 == white && f_X_pp1_pp2 == white && f_X_pp1_pp3 == white && f_X_pp1_pp4 == white && f_X_pp1_pp5 == white) && (f_X_pp2_pp1 == black && f_X_pp2_pp2 == white && f_X_pp2_pp3 == white && f_X_pp2_pp4 == white && f_X_pp2_pp5 == white) && (f_X_pp3_pp1 == black && f_X_pp3_pp2 == black && f_X_pp3_pp3 == empty && f_X_pp3_pp4 == white && f_X_pp3_pp5 == white) && (f_X_pp4_pp1 == black && f_X_pp4_pp2 == black && f_X_pp4_pp3 == black && f_X_pp4_pp4 == black && f_X_pp4_pp5 == white) && f_X_pp5_pp1 == black && f_X_pp5_pp2 == black && f_X_pp5_pp3 == black && f_X_pp5_pp4 == black && f_X_pp5_pp5 == black)) ->
